- **experiment-rigor**: THE differentiator — decide an A/B test correctly and REFUSE to ship a fake winner. SRM check first, peeking made structurally impossible (Bayesian/sequential), pre-registration as an eval contract, multiple-comparison correction, CUPED-only-with-covariate, bounded retention. Use when asked "is this A/B test significant", "can I ship the winner", "did my experiment win", "check this experiment", or "set up an A/B test". Encodes tally's O5/O6/O8/O9 + J5 + BTS-4/5/16/17 + P13–P17. ([naman10parikh/tally](https://github.com/naman10parikh/tally/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-rigor))
